    Expecting ARIA children role to be added: group, menuitemradio, menuitem, menuitemcheckbox, menu, separator

    Element's background color could not be determined due to a background image

    A model read these as failing, but no fact Rampa checks supports the reading: on real pages such readings were almost all wrong. They are suggestions for a person to decide, never failures.
  ? WCAG 2.5.8 (AA) — Target Size (Minimum): 2 elements · target-size
    Target has insufficient space to its closest neighbors. Safe clickable space has a diameter of 17px instead of at least 24px. Is the neighbor a target?
